[2026-09-07] Avstämning v2 redesign folded into UI v2 PR 8 (registers) instead of a tenth stacked PR: the cutover PR's base would have had to move, and the Konton table it sits next to is in the same PR.
[2026-09-07] WooCommerce wc-auth return_url is built on the initiating request's trusted origin (resolveRequestAppOrigin, brands-table validated, canonical on unknown host or lookup failure) while callback_url stays on the canonical app URL: sessions are per domain, so a white-label user returned to the canonical host hit the initiator check with no session and a foreign-branded login. No stored origin column and no provider_otc handoff (PR #2305 pattern) needed, because unlike a registered OAuth redirect URI the wc-auth return_url is free-form per handshake and can simply point at the brand host; the return route resolves its redirect base from the host it was reached on the same way.
[2026-09-07] PR #2373 review: the Skatteverket callback now peeks the flow row for its initiator and binds the session BEFORE consuming state or handoff (Superagent P2: a DELETE-before-auth let a signed-out or wrong-user arrival burn a live consent). A session-less arrival is sent to /login on the initiating origin and resumes into the same URL; a wrong user is refused with the row left claimable. This reverses the earlier 'no login resume' decision for this flow; the consume stays the atomic gate, the peek only decides who may attempt it. Handoff TTL raised from two to five minutes to fit a sign-in.
[2026-09-07] BankID confirmation mail and the Send Email hook resolve their link host through lib/domains/trusted-app-origin instead of the raw forwarded host / GoTrue's redirect_to: the BankID mail is the one auth link GoTrue's redirect allowlist never sees (built here, sent via Resend), and the hook's signature proves the sender, not the destination, while the GoTrue allowlist is a hand-configured glob. Unknown, lookalike, credential-bearing, non-default-port and malformed destinations collapse to the canonical /auth/callback with no next path; a registered brand host over http is upgraded to https. Brand sender identity is resolved from the RESOLVED host so mail branding and link destination always agree. A brands-table read failure refuses (BankID: step resolve_origin, signup rolls back; hook: 500 so Supabase retries) rather than mailing a canonical link to a white-label user. Dropped from the audit's plan 7 as already in place after #2376: signup route, HTTPS enforcement, credential/port checks, recovery/invite/email-change coverage.
[2026-09-07] Booking flow anchors on the underlag (founder-approved): the review shows the document from either door (pinned document_id, else the inbox item matched to the row), states the moms it carries and books that figure by default when it disagrees with the rate by more than 1 kr (ML 8 kap. 17 §, the deduction follows the invoice). Only rate-based treatments are overridden; counterparty patterns and reverse charge keep their lines. Preview, Ändra rader and the server share one adjustment (applyVatAmountToLines / applyVatAmountOverride) so the review never shows a figure it does not book.
[2026-09-07] Missing-underlag prompt threshold = 500 kr (UNDERLAG_PROMPT_THRESHOLD_SEK): every verifikat needs an underlag by law, but prompting before every coffee is what makes people stop booking; above the threshold the review says "Inget underlag" with "Hämta underlag" and the button reads "Bokför utan underlag". A blocking gate was rejected: booking now and completing later is the honest path, not a refusal.
[2026-09-09] Skatteverket reconnect line on Transaktioner only under the Skatteverket source filter (reverses part of 2026-08-25): the Skattekonto page carries the reconnect notice itself, and a line on every bank-list visit read as nagging.
[2026-09-09] One template picker, one vocabulary: the transactions TemplatePicker is the only picker (bank row, supplier invoice line, Ny verifikation, Bokför direkt, Bokför från mall) and library templates are filed into the catalog's families by account (template-groups.ts); the booking_template_library.category column stays as a derived machine field (vat routes the series) and is no longer chosen by hand: founder call, the second vocabulary (EU-handel/Skattekonto/Lön) read as a different product.
[2026-09-09] Inköp lands on Leverantörsfakturor and /purchases redirects there: the v2 landing page with two flow strips said less than the invoice list itself (founder: "maybe this page is not needed at all"); PurchasesFlow stays in the tree for the strips' next home, if any.
[2026-09-10] Granska bokföring opens with one recommendation header (pick, why, Byt) and the assistant as one line under it, not a separate box: the AI block was hidden exactly when a template was set, so the person got a preview with no reason; a rule or a learned counterpart is never overridden by the model, and with a template the assistant's alternative opens the picker on that account instead of setting an override the booking never read.
[2026-09-10] Stripe fees book under reverse charge (template payment_fees_eu, 6570 + ruta 21/30/48) and no longer as VAT-free bank fees: the catalog keyword 'stripe' contradicted the Stripe extension's own payout booking (payouts.ts); Klarna, Zettle, Swish, Nets stay on bank_fees since their VAT status was not verified. Intern representation moved from 7622 (Sjuk- och hälsovård, ej avdragsgill) to 7631 with reduced_12 VAT, the 2017 rules in the note.
[2026-09-10] The assistant reads unbooked rows ahead of the person (transaction_assistant_reads + ten-minute cron) and known rows book from the row's Bokför with Ångra (rule; counterpart seen >= 3; assistant >= 0.8 with a receipt): founder chose both after the review header showed the read arriving ten seconds after the dialog. Undo stays the storno path the toast already had; a delayed commit was rejected because a closed tab would silently drop bookings. Klarna/Zettle/Swish stay on the review since only Stripe's treatment was verified.
[2026-09-10] Mapping-rule templates that did not match the row are 'recent' (picker only, confidence 0.1), no longer 'rule' at 0.85: with row chips in v2 the old boost made every expense row wear the last rule's template and would have let direct booking post it. A row without a match shows Välj kategori instead.
[2026-09-10] One booking proposal (lib/bookkeeping/proposal.ts) for every source and surface, with the booking field as one of the three API shapes: founder chose simplicity and accuracy after the review's per-branch state booked 5890 under a 6071 header. Category suggestions and merchant history dropped from the UI and the assistant's slate (counterpart rules carry that memory); the MCP suggest tool returns the same proposals with categorize_args so Claude books what a person sees. Multi-line counterpart patterns stay app-only for agents (categorize_args null) until the MCP categorize tool accepts a counterparty rule id.
[2026-09-07] ROT/RUT avslag reopens the original invoice instead of issuing a new one: the refused share is not new revenue but a fordran that moves from Skatteverket (1513) to the buyer (1510), so a new invoice would book revenue and moms a second time; the reopen keeps one fordran per job and the payment matcher works unchanged. The invoice document is left as issued (deduction_total untouched) and the refused share lives in a new invoices.deduction_reclaimed_total column; the customer share formula in customer-share.ts and its SQL twin gained the same term. A multi-invoice beslut recorded as one total (PATCH partially_paid) is refused with SPLIT_UNKNOWN rather than split pro rata: the split is Skatteverket's, the beslutsfil carries it.
[2026-09-07] ROT/RUT overview page (/invoices/rot-rut) instead of growing the file dialog: begäran, beslut import, utbetalning and nekat belopp are one lifecycle and the dialog was action-only. The 31 January begäran deadline stays where it is (tax deadlines + file-time notice); no second reminder surface. A company default RUT work type was dropped from the plan: articles already carry housework_type, and the invoice editor already falls back to the customer's stored personnummer.
[2026-09-07] MCP: gnubok_list_rot_rut_payout_requests ships search-only (READ, bridged by gnubok_call_tool); gnubok_settle_rot_rut_payout stays in the default catalog as a WRITE and the tools/list ceiling moved 60K to 60.5K (measured 60 428) rather than demoting a read without usage data. The dashboard match route and the MCP commit executor share one pre-flight + settle in lib/invoices/rot-rut-match-transaction.ts. No MCP reclaim tool yet: the reclaim is a dashboard action until an agent asks for it.
[2026-09-07] Klarmarkera (markPeriodClosedExternally) now asks whether the period has any lines on result accounts (BAS class 3-8) instead of whether it has any non-imported verifikat at all. The old test was a proxy for the thing the guard protects (3xxx-8xxx transferred by a bokslutsverifikat, BFL 5-6 kap) and it shut out the migrated first year whose only native voucher re-keys the opening balance (1930/2081 aktiekapital) after a failed SIE import; with the next year's IB already imported the normal year-end refused too (NEXT_PERIOD_HAS_IB), so the year had no closing path at all (EHAL AB support case 2026-09-07). Existence check via id-only entry fetch plus per-chunk head counts with early exit, no line fetch, no journal_entries!inner embed. Same session: the loaded årsredovisning view got the FyPicker in its header, because the no-period branch auto-jumps to the remembered scope before its own picker is ever seen, which left users with no way to reach another year's årsredovisning.
[2026-09-07] Klarmarkera's new balance-sheet-only leg additionally requires the NEXT period to already carry IB (opening_balance_entry_id set). Without that, a natively bookkept year with only balance-sheet vouchers (dormant AB with just the aktiekapital deposit) could be klarmarkerad and its balances would never reach the next year, since klarmarkera skips the IB posting the normal year-end does. With the IB in place the normal year-end refuses (NEXT_PERIOD_HAS_IB) and klarmarkera is the only path; without it the normal year-end works and is the right path. Raised by the swedish-compliance bot on PR #2393 (its suggested alternative, a dedicated IB-correction tag on entries, was rejected: it adds a concept for the user to know about where the ledger already answers the question). Same round: the result-account range is bounded to class 3-8 (lt '9'), class 9 interna poster are not transferred by a bokslut.
[2026-09-08] Enable Banking's bank_transaction_code object ({description, code, sub_code}) is flattened to a string by one helper in the connect-contract file (normalizeBankTransactionCode: code, code/sub_code, else description), applied by both producers (Connect's normalizeBookedTransaction, the ledger's convertTransaction), and the wire schema stays z.string().nullable(). Rejected: widening the contract to string | object and normalizing only in the ledger's sync.ts. It keeps the wire type dishonest, leaves Connect's own label derivation reading an object, and still needs the direct-path fix, because that path had been writing the object's JSON text into transactions.bank_transaction_code for 78 companies since 2026-08-09 while Connect failed the same type lie loudly on every canary sync from 2026-09-03 (Capstone support case, 2026-09-07). The helper lives in the contract despite its "shape, never behaviour" rule because the two repos already mirror that file byte for byte; a copy per producer is the drift that caused the outage. Repair migration rewrites the stored JSON text with the same rule and nothing else (no transaction_method re-derivation).
[2026-09-07] PR #2397 skeptic + review pass: the reclaim is refused while any of its invoices sits in a later live begäran (avslag → new file is Skatteverket's retry; booking the refused share onto the customer meanwhile would clear 1513 twice), a reclaimed invoice is blocked from a new begäran (DEDUCTION_RECLAIMED) until the reclaim voucher is reversed, and a storno of the reclaim voucher syncs the invoices and the begäran back (lib/invoices/rot-rut-reclaim-reversal.ts, hooked into reverseEntry next to the payment sync). Per-invoice reopen goes through one idempotent RPC (apply_rot_rut_reclaim_invoice, item marker + invoice row in one transaction) so a failure after the voucher is resumable instead of stuck at ALREADY_DONE. Crediting an invoice with a reclaimed share is refused (reverse the reclaim first): the credit note reverses the issue-time 1510/1513 split. Declined: CONCURRENTLY for the partial unique index (Supabase migrations run in one transaction; same shape as the shipped rot_rut_payout index in 20260904021000, partial predicate on a source_type that few rows match).
[2026-09-08] PR #2397 review cycle 3: the reclaim RPCs own the accounting values. apply_rot_rut_reclaim_invoice takes only the refused share and validates it against the locked item, request and invoice (never above the item's requested amount, the beslut's refused total, or the 1513 headroom), then derives remaining_amount and status from the same formula as the INSERT guard (rot_rut_customer_outstanding); revert_rot_rut_reclaim_invoice mirrors it for a reversed reclaim voucher and the request link is cleared only after every leg succeeded. Reason: a SECURITY INVOKER function that accepted caller-supplied remaining/status was an unchecked accounting write for any writer-role member (CWE-862). The 20260907160300 signature is dropped in 20260907160400 rather than edited: the preview branch had already applied it.
[2026-09-08] Ideell förening (#2072 step 1) lands as a third EntityType only after every form-dependent fact was routed through lib/company/entity-type.ts (byEntityType with Record arms, resolveCompanyEntityType instead of `?? 'enskild_firma'` / `?? 'aktiebolag'`): widening the union alone compiled everywhere and changed nothing, so a förening would have booked as EF in the app and as AB in bokslut and MCP. 28 silent-default sites replaced; the only compile error the widening produced was one Record<EntityType>.
[2026-09-08] Förening domain facts, chosen from the BAS data in the repo since the compliance skill has no förening chapter (verify with an accountant before Phase 2): result closes to 2069 and the year-open omföring carries it to 2068 (the same result-appropriation service as AB 2099/2098, generalised on resultClosingAccounts; the skeptic showed 2069 would otherwise accumulate across years); no owner accounts, member settlement on 2890 (EF 2013/2018, AB 2893) in category mapping, booking templates (owner accounts in a template's base column translate to 2890), expense claims and privately paid supplier invoices; helårsmoms deadline follows the räkenskapsår schedule like an AB (SFL 26 kap 33 §); accrual default; brutet räkenskapsår allowed (only EF is calendar-locked, BFL 3 kap 1 §); K1 label for the 5 000 kr accrual threshold (BFNAR 2010:1); org number gets the 16 prefix; personnel accounts auto-created on first payroll as for EF. No INK3, no årsbokslut, no förening packs, no deadlines yet: Phase 2 on the tracker.
[2026-09-08] Ideell förening creation is behind NEXT_PUBLIC_IDEELL_FORENING_ENABLED (one flag, read by the onboarding picker and the shared CompanySetupSchema/actions gate) rather than open from day one: the DB CHECK, RPCs and chart seed accept the value regardless, so the first partner (SS Gambit via Roslagens Webbyrå) can be switched on without a migration and the flag can be dropped once Phase 2 lands. stiftelse deliberately not bundled: identical plumbing, no chart block.
[2026-09-08] Invoice list gets an 'Ej skickade' view and the PDF download on an unissued document asks first (#2399): both reports came from the same hidden state, a finalized invoice with an F-number whose DB status is still 'draft'. Chosen: split the status in the UI (lib/invoices/invoice-list-tabs.ts, one predicate for rows, counts and sections) and gate the download with a soft dialog whose primary action is the existing manual mark-sent (and book) path, then download. Rejected: a real 'issued' status in the DB (touches MCP, v1 API, reports and SIE for a distinction invoice_number already carries); removing the UTKAST stamp from numbered drafts (an unbooked invoice is not issued, the stamp is right, the flow around it was wrong); naming the tab 'Godkända' as the user asked (there is no attest step, so the label would promise one; ?status=godkanda aliases to the view).
[2026-09-08] Supplier org_number identity (#2391): one lenient 10-digit key (orgNumberKey in lib/invariants/org-number.ts, digits only, last 10 of 12, no Luhn) now drives the exact matcher, the extractor's self-invoice guard, the web/v1 schemas and the MCP create tool, plus a backfill that strips formatting from existing rows. Luhn stays lenient on suppliers: two rows with the same mistyped number are one supplier, and parties is Luhn-strict at promotion already. Declined: the unique index on (company_id, org_number) in the same PR, because prod holds 94 duplicate groups under the canonical key (18 companies, one group of 124 rows that looks like a placeholder) that need a merge decision first; follow-up issue instead. Declined: rejecting non-Swedish shapes on write, because 68 prod rows hold foreign registration numbers in the column and eu/non-eu suppliers would become unwritable. parties is the end state for counterparty identity: no further constraints go on suppliers beyond this.
[2026-09-08] #2391 skeptic pass: orgNumberKey only strips hyphens and spaces and only unprefixes 12-digit values behind 16/18/19/20. Reason: 26 prod supplier rows hold a VAT number (orgnr + 01, prefixes 55/52/87) in org_number, and 'last 10 of any 12 digits' would have rewritten them to another company's identity; letters stay because BE0123456789 is not the Swedish 0123456789. The matcher scans live suppliers only (archived_at IS NULL), the list and v1 search compare without separators, the CSV import and the provider migration orchestrator key and write through the same rule.
[2026-09-08] correctEntry re-points the original entry's transaction_voucher_links rows to the corrected entry (lib/core/bookkeeping/storno-service.ts relinkTransactionsToEntry) instead of deleting them as issue #2364 proposed. Why: for a samlingsverifikat (bulk-book N>1) the junction is the row's only anchor, so deleting it would push rows the corrected verifikat still explains back into Att bokföra; the pointer column already follows the correction and the junction now follows it the same way, so every reader (is_transaction_booked, fetchJunctionLinkedTxIds, the bulk_book RPC) sees one live anchor. Rejected: a relink_entry_anchors RPC moving pointer and junction atomically (a migration plus pg test for a path that is already best-effort across five other statements; revisit if a partial failure ever shows up in the surfaced transactionRelinkError). Prod repair (planned, runs after merge on the founder's go; completion gets its own dated entry): the 7 stale links (3 companies) all sit on rows whose pointer names a posted entry (4 on a correction chain, 3 from a June 2026 samlingsverifikat storno that predates the junction cleanup and were re-booked 1:1); they will be re-pointed to the pointer's entry, the same rule the fix applies, rather than deleted.
[2026-09-08] delete_last_voucher returns a correction's bank anchors (transactions.journal_entry_id and transaction_voucher_links rows) to correction_of_id before the row is deleted (migration 20260908095907). Why: the #2364 skeptic showed that once the junction follows the correction, the two-step undo (delete the correction, then the storno) cascaded the links away and restored an original that explains bank rows nobody points at, so the rows surfaced as bookable again; before, the links had stayed on the original by accident. Chosen over releasing the rows (the restored original would still explain them, same trap) and over a TS pre-step in the DELETE route (not atomic with the RPC's own guards: a refused delete would leave anchors on a reversed entry). A duplicate of a link the original already holds is dropped, not re-pointed (UNIQUE (transaction_id, journal_entry_id)).

[2026-09-08] Zettle v1 is paid purchases (and refunds) into webshop_orders via the Purchase API, Shopify-shaped feed-only: partner-hosted OAuth with rotating refresh token (encrypted), unpaid IZETTLE_INVOICE-only purchases skipped, line items + groupedVatAmounts as booking underlag. Finance API payouts/fees deliberately out of scope (phase 2, Stripe settlement style).
[2026-09-08] Zettle orders cron pages candidates (range) and caps entitled syncs at 50 instead of limit(50) before hasCapability: entitlement skips must not consume the batch or advance last_order_synced_at (purchase recovery cursor). Declined a separate cron_checked_at column for now; revisit if scanned non-entitled volume becomes a time-budget problem.
[2026-09-08] #2395 missing-underlag: split the supplier-invoice .or() into two .in() lookups instead of halving LOOKUP_CHUNK or moving the list filters into the verifikat_without_documents RPC: halving only moves the proxy-limit wall; the RPC move deletes the TS mirror (one predicate instead of two) but pulls search, series, date and sort into SQL and is the surface owner's design call. Intended next step, not taken here.
[2026-09-08] Onboarding name search picks via chip row, never the top hit blind: Typesense name ranking is fuzzy and common names ("Bygg AB", sole-trader surnames) make a blind pick a wrong company; five hits, active first, fired on Enter only to protect the 3000/mo TIC budget (issue #2418).
[2026-09-08] #2418 skeptic pass: a name-search hit's org number is derived from the Lens registrationNumber via lensRegistrationToOrgNumber (16-prefixed 12 digits and the 16-digit enskild-firma form, century plus 4-digit serial, reduce to the 10-digit key; hits that do not normalize are dropped) instead of being stored as returned. Why: the typed-orgnr path never stores Lens's number, so this was the first place a 16-digit value reached settings.org_number and createCompany refused it at the last step. Sole-trader chips name the form ("Enskild firma") instead of the number, because that number is the owner's personnummer and the repo masks those everywhere else; the number still travels in the search payload since a pick has to store it.
[2026-09-08] Medelantal anställda (Not 2, ÅRL 5:20 §) gets a whole-number override on arsredovisning_narratives (migration 20260908130127) instead of the free-text note override the support request asked for. Why: the number keeps the statutory sentence and the iXBRL MedelantaletAnstallda fact correct; free text would let a non-compliant note through and could not be tagged. One resolver (lib/salary/medelantal.ts resolveMedelantalAnstallda: override, else FTE average over employees) feeds the K2 and K3 note builders and the iXBRL input, which also reads the previous period's override so the jämförelseår column shows the same figure the previous year's document did. Rejected: rounding 0.5 up globally (silently changes every company's note and does nothing for the 148 of 195 aktiebolag with salary but no employees rows); asking the user to backdate employment_start (fixes one company, misstates the hire date).
[2026-09-08] Issue #2413 BAS 2026 kontogrupp 12: kept 1249/1259/1269 in the catalog renamed after their free heads and dropped only 1241/1242/1251/1261, instead of removing all seven retired sub-accounts and moving the asset module's vehicle/computer defaults to BAS 2026 (1226/1224 on 1229): the asset module's DEFAULT_ACCOUNTS_BY_CATEGORY still books vehicles on 1240/1249 and computers on 1250/1259 (31 live assets in prod, guard test requires the triple in BAS_REFERENCE), so dropping the contra accounts would have forced a depreciation-default change into a label fix; that change is the founder's call and lives in #2414. The prod backfill renames only the exact catalog literal next to a free-labelled head, so old-BAS imports (1240 Bilar + 1249 Ack. avskr. bilar) and user renames stay untouched.
[2026-09-08] Migration files must carry their own BEGIN/COMMIT when they use transaction-only statements (LOCK TABLE, SET LOCAL, SET CONSTRAINTS): CI replays each file with psql -f in autocommit and the Supabase branch runner does the same on prod, so the bare LOCK TABLE in 20260908113353 (#2413, PR #2419) failed both and stalled prod's migration queue behind it. Prod never recorded the failed version, so the file was deleted and re-issued as 20260908120449 rather than edited in place.
[2026-09-08] Issue #2196 Nyckeltal per month: the month table (income, expenses, net) is ON by default and toggled by a boolean preference showMonthlyTable instead of a KPI_DEFINITIONS entry. Why: every stored preferences row already carries a complete kpiOrder array, and KPIPanes renders from that array, so a new definition id would stay hidden for every existing company no matter its defaultVisible; a boolean absent from the stored JSON resolves through mergeWithDefaults on read. The payload has carried income and expenses per month since the RPC (only net was rendered after the Recharts chart was dropped), so no API or migration change. #2185's multi-year view stays separate: it needs several fiscal periods fetched side by side, which this page does not do.
[2026-09-08] #2425 ROT/RUT: the picker's begäran rows keep single-click handoff and gain a checkbox per row for a bundled pick, instead of a new multi-select mode or an ambiguity-tolerant matcher. Why: the server (request_ids, settleRotRutPayoutRequestSet) and the confirm dialog already take N begäran since #2360; only the manual fallback was 1:1, and a firm whose jobs share a price hits the matcher's ambiguity refusal often. Guessing between two same-amount begäran would clear the wrong 1513 fordran, so the choice stays with the user.
[2026-09-08] Issue #2426 ROT/RUT begäran state in the invoice list: normal states (Att begära, Skapad, Uppladdad, Beviljad) render as muted text and only Delvis beviljad and Avslagen get a chip (design convention 5: a fönsterputsfirma where every invoice is ROT/RUT would otherwise carry the same chip on every row); the filter is a third ContextPicker (?rotrut=) gated on rot_rut_enabled or an invoiced deduction, not extra entries in the status view, so a ROT/RUT state can be combined with any status view and companies without ROT/RUT never see it. One predicate (lib/invoices/rot-rut-list-status.ts) feeds the column, the filter and its counts; the state is read from a rot_rut_payout_request_items embed on the existing list query, no new API and no migration.
[2026-09-08] Receipt purchase date: fixed the extraction prompt's description of invoice.invoiceDate (schema comment plus an explicit "invoiceDate on receipts" rule) instead of adding a receipt-specific purchaseDate field. Why: prod (last 30 days) showed receipts losing the date on 46% of items (75% via WhatsApp) while supplier invoices lost it on 0.4%, and purchaseTime was filled on nearly every affected receipt: the date was described as a bare ISO field under the invoice block beside a purchaseTime rule marked "receipts only", and the model read that as "invoice-only". A new field would need a schema change, a UI column and every consumer (WhatsApp M4 ack date, the Bokför entry-date default that falls back to today, the worklist) taught a second date; the prompt fix stops the class with no data-shape change. Existing undated items are not re-extracted by this; a backfill is a separate call.
[2026-09-08] Onboarding act two (#2438) lives at /onboarding/books under app/(dashboard) with a bare journey shell in the dashboard layout, not in the (onboarding) route group: the SIE wizard, the migration workspace and the bank panel all read CompanyProvider and reference data, so hosting them outside the dashboard layout would mean a second company context or a rewrite. The first-session gate is a 24 h cookie (gnubok-books-gate) checked in middleware, not a company_settings column: no hot-path DB read, and a returning user is never trapped by a stale flag. The old branch step ("Jag ser mig omkring först") is removed: 3 of 30 companies that chose it were active after week one.
[2026-09-08] Assistant and earlier räkenskapsår (#2185 part 3): the MCP report tools resolve the fiscal period from a date argument (from_date, to_date, as_of_date) when no period_id is given, instead of defaulting to the most recent period and then rejecting the date; the in-app assistant's snapshot and the chat identity block list the company's räkenskapsår with period_id (lib/agent/fiscal-years.ts) plus one rule on addressing them. No fiscal_year parameter was added to the six report schemas because tools/list sits at its 60 000 token ceiling, and the range guard was kept because it was a deliberate fix for silently dropped ranges.
[2026-09-08] #2187 (a) Att göra row "Betala in till Skatteverket" shows the shortfall (next charge minus the last synced saldo, the full charge only when no snapshot exists) and appears only when something has to be paid in, instead of a deadline-style row for every upcoming charge: a company whose saldo covers the dragning has nothing to do, and the /skattekonto page's Nästa dragning math already defines the number, now computed once in lib/worklist for Hem and /api/worklist/counts. Part (b), a betalfil for the skattekonto payment, stays a follow-up: the existing payment-file route (app/api/skatteverket/tax-payments/[period]/payment-file) is AGI-scoped (amount from agi_declarations, reachable only from a salary run) and needs an amount source before it can pay a general dragning.
[2026-09-08] Issue #2224 offert -> kundorder: the quote source was added to the existing proforma -> order service (lib/sales-orders/convert-to-sales-order.ts) and to gnubok_convert_invoice as a target parameter staged under the existing convert_invoice operation type, instead of a second service, a separate MCP tool and a new pending-operation type. Why: one conversion path and one guard pair (a quote with a live order cannot become an invoice, a quote with a live invoice cannot become an order) keep the proforma and quote flows identical, and reusing the operation type keeps the pending-operations type list untouched. The quote flips to accepted like quote -> invoice does; cancelling it like the proforma path would hide the customer's accepted agreement. Skeptic refutation: for an already-accepted quote that accepted -> accepted compare-and-set cannot detect a concurrent conversion, so the "never twice" guard moved into the database (migration 20260908165000: one live kundorder per source, and two triggers that lock the quote row and refuse a live order beside a live converted invoice and vice versa); the service pre-checks stay for the friendly error. Second refutation: order -> invoice booked a foreign-currency invoice 1:1 when Riksbanken had no rate (pre-existing, but the quote now depends on that path and the fail-closed quote -> invoice route is refused while an order lives), so createInvoiceFromSalesOrder now fails closed with SALES_ORDER_INVOICE_FX_RATE_UNAVAILABLE. Locking the quote decision (open/declined) while a live order exists was left out: the DB trigger only knows about converted invoices and changing it is a migration; a declined quote with a live order is harmless since the order carries the sale.
[2026-09-08] Issue #2224 follow-up from the correctness skeptic: the quote decision (open/declined) is now locked in the database while a live kundorder exists (migration 20260908165100 extends invoices_quote_decision_guard), reversing the earlier call to leave it open; a declined offert behind a confirmed, invoiced order was a contradictory agreement trail and the dashboard hid the re-accept button, so the quote was stuck. The three source and decision guards run as SECURITY DEFINER: a SELECT FOR UPDATE under RLS admits only the caller's active company, so a multi-company member writing for another company through raw PostgREST got no row, no lock and no guard. Both landed as a second migration rather than an edit of 20260908165000, which was already applied to staging under that version.
[2026-09-08] Draft invoice PDF marks a draft with one diagonal, faint word (UTKAST / DRAFT) across every page instead of a banner in the top margin (#2437): a banner reads as UI chrome on a document, a watermark reads as a stamp and leaves the preview pixel-identical to the final print. The long legal sentence (saknar löpnummer, ML 17 kap 24 §) is dropped on purpose: the word alone says the document is not a valid invoice, and the download dialog (#2399) already explains why before the file exists. Rotation and opacity sit on a padded wrapper View so the word turns about its own centre. Skeptic refutation accepted: the first cut (#6b7280 at 0.14, about 92% brightness) would drop out of a monochrome print or greyscale scan, and a numbered draft otherwise prints title, number and OCR like a real faktura; now #4b5563 at 0.3 (about 79% brightness), with a test pinning the composited grey between 70% and 85%. A 1-bit scan can still threshold the word away; a second explicit line on numbered drafts was left out because the request was the word alone, and that residual is Emil's call. Second refutation accepted: the overlay is emitted as the LAST child of the Page, because react-pdf paints in document order and `fixed` does not hoist, so an overlay emitted first was painted under the opaque payment and customer boxes and the word vanished on the page that carries totals and OCR; a test now inflates the PDF content streams and asserts the glyph run comes after the last rectangle fill on every page. BETALD and MAKULERAD banners are left as they are.
[2026-09-08] Negative journal-line amounts: fixed the sign at three levels (producers flip the SIDE via lib/bookkeeping/line-side.ts, the engine refuses negative amounts before any write, and a NOT VALID CHECK on journal_entry_lines) instead of only patching the supplier-invoice generator or hiding negative items in the form. Why: the invariant lived nowhere (no Zod rule, no engine check, no constraint), so MCP, templates and any future producer could repeat it; negative items themselves are valid input (rabatt, öresavrundning), so rejecting them at input would break real invoices. reverseEntry now swaps on the net so legacy negative lines storno cleanly before the data repair runs.
[2026-09-08] Björn Lundén migration killed at the function ceiling (Well Done Accounting, 9 415 customer invoices): fixed at four levels instead of only raising maxDuration. (1) BL batch pages are 1 000 rows (production-verified: `rows` honored up to 10 000, 1.4 s a page) instead of 50, so the register is 10 requests, not 189; (2) the invoice steps list first, drop what is already in the database, and hydrate only the rest, so a re-run spends the detail budget on missing rows instead of the same open invoices; (3) the run carries a deadline (660 s budget under an 800 s ceiling, the Pro maximum) and the hydration budget is derived from what is left minus a per-row insert reserve, so a run that cannot finish still hands back its terminal NDJSON line instead of dying mid-stream; (4) one timing log line per invoice step, because the killed runs logged nothing for 300 s. Found on the way and fixed in the same change: BL's /customer and /supplier answer a bare array that getPage read as an empty envelope, so every BL migration imported zero customers and suppliers and rebuilt both as invoice stubs; the register steps now honor the config's paginated flag, and a register row folds onto an org-less stub with the same name (filling the org number) so re-running an already-migrated company does not duplicate its parties. Not done: per-page progress events inside a step (the stream is silent for up to a few minutes on a large register) and splitting the migration into resumable requests; both are the next step if a register turns up that does not fit 660 s.
[2026-09-08] Counterpart resolver = authority record + bands, not a confirm queue: parties stay the record, counterparty_aliases holds the bank-side variants (display-time overlay, verifikat text untouched), link/tentative/nil bands at 0.8/0.6, cheap-tier model reads each new string once with register candidates and a verify pass, shared directory takes a reading only when two companies produced it. Why: no international product confirms merchant records; 4 040 suggested parties sat unconfirmed across 31 companies; Haiku read 200 prod strings at ~96 % high-band precision for 8 cents.
[2026-09-08] Motparter page = one flat list (party or resolver reading, marks, what they sell, account, rows, in, out, last), no Förslag/Bara i bokföringen tabs, actions on the row menu; suggestions take the directory's brand name, confirmed records keep theirs. Why: the confirm queue was not worked (4 040 unconfirmed rows fleet-wide) and no international product has one; unnamed spend belongs on the transactions, not on a counterpart list.
[2026-09-09] SIE import account creation (syncMappedAccounts) inserts in chunks of 100 rows instead of one statement per chart, and executeSIEImport closes its pending sie_imports row in a finally block on every exit: a full-BAS Bokio import (1 242 accounts) hit the authenticated role's 8 s statement_timeout on prod (8.2 s cancelled for one company, 6.5 s passed for the next), and the early `return result` after the failed account sync left the row 'pending', so the user's retry 40 s later was refused by the file-hash unique index with a message that named "gnubok" and an "Ersätt import" button that does not exist. Chunking was chosen over moving the insert into an RPC with SET statement_timeout (the import_sie_journal_entries precedent): the insert is idempotent per row, a partial success is safe to retry, and no migration or pg-real ratchet is needed; the per-row trigger cost (audit log, writer-role guard, RLS WITH CHECK) is untouched and stays a separate concern. The slot-held message is static (no lookup of the holder) so it stays deterministic in the queued-mock tests.
[2026-09-09] Onboarding search-as-you-type (#2448) reads SCB's företagsregister per debounced keystroke and runs TIC once, on the pick, instead of TIC per keystroke: the 3000/month Lens budget is why #2421 fired on Enter only, and SCB is free; the pick still needs TIC because SCB knows no F-skatt, VAT or fiscal year. No rate limit on either route, founder's call (2026-09-09): the debounce, the 3-char minimum and the abort of superseded requests are the only throttles. Sole traders are included in the onboarding search (SCB legal form 10) but stay excluded from the parties picker; the row names the form, never the personnummer.
[2026-09-09] Bank picker: an UNCHECKED account holds its bokföringskonto only as a soft claim (yields to a checked account that wants it), instead of either a hard claim (status quo: 400 "Flera bankkonton kan inte bokföras på samma konto", the support dead end where 1930 could never move from the wrong bank account to the right one because the picker hides the ledger dropdown for unchecked rows and disconnect + reconnect re-claims the same rows by IBAN) or a full release on every save (fewer states, but it demotes every unchecked row to a manual ghost holding its ledger and loses the "re-check lands back on the same account" prefill). Contested rows are demoted to manual in ONE update before the mirror, and upsertFromPsd2 then promotes the manual holder in place, which keeps row ids, transactions.cash_account_id links and the is_primary flag on the 1930 row; the same release pass also makes two checked accounts swapping ledgers work (previously both upserts tripped the unique constraint and were swallowed per-account). Another connection's UNCHECKED row yields the same way (cash_accounts.enabled = false), matching how session sharing already counts only enabled rows as claims; a synced-elsewhere row stays a 400. No new client/UI: the picker needed no change once the server stopped counting unchecked rows.

[2026-09-08] Zettle v1 = paid purchases→webshop_orders via Purchase API; Finance payouts out of scope; OAuth refresh-token store; unpaid IZETTLE_INVOICE-only skipped.
[2026-09-08] Zettle v1 is paid purchases (and refunds) into webshop_orders via the Purchase API, Shopify-shaped feed-only: partner-hosted OAuth with rotating refresh token (encrypted), unpaid IZETTLE_INVOICE-only purchases skipped, line items + groupedVatAmounts as booking underlag. Finance API payouts/fees deliberately out of scope (phase 2, Stripe settlement style).
[2026-09-08] Zettle orders cron pages candidates (range) and caps entitled syncs at 50 instead of limit(50) before hasCapability: entitlement skips must not consume the batch or advance last_order_synced_at (purchase recovery cursor). Declined a separate cron_checked_at column for now; revisit if scanned non-entitled volume becomes a time-budget problem.
[2026-09-09] PR #2416 (Zettle, community-authored) adopted on the contributor's branch instead of re-implemented: kept the Shopify-shaped feed-only design, added migration 20260909100400 for the sites that enumerate platforms/connection tables (platform CHECKs on webshop_orders and webshop_store_settings, writer-role gate trigger, migration-reset snapshot and lock via the 20260826150000 wrapper pattern rather than re-issuing the 400-line reset body), renamed the four PR migrations past prod's 20260908143051, froze the validated connect origin on zettle_connections.return_origin so white-label users return to their brand domain (Zettle has one registered callback URL), and derive per-rate VAT net from Zettle's own product rows (tax / rate drifted from what was charged) with a one-öre-per-row line-sum tolerance instead of 0.005 kr (silently dropped multi-row 12%/6% underlag). Per-purchase rows kept for v1; daily kassarapport aggregation and Finance API fees/payouts are the follow-up.
[2026-09-09] Zettle v1 imports split-tender, gift-card (sale or tender) and tip-carrying purchases as is_paid = false rows titled 'bokför manuellt' and skips their refunds, instead of booking them through the one-account / revenue-per-rate model: the skeptic showed a card+cash split would put the whole gross on 1686, a card+invoice split would count as paid, a 0 % gift-card row would land on 3004 / ruta 42 (it is a 2421 liability), and tips would book as momsfri sale. Proper support needs per-payment amounts and a voucher liability on webshop_orders (follow-up). Sync runs claim the connection (sync_lock_until) before refreshing the rotating token: a concurrent cron + manual sync otherwise reuses a refresh token, Zettle answers 400, and the connection flips to revoked. The cron snapshots its candidate list before syncing because each sync moves the row to the tail of the last_order_synced_at ordering, so live offset paging re-fetched synced rows and skipped unseen ones.
[2026-09-09] SIE #BTRANS/#RTRANS history (#2427) lands in journal_entry_rattelse_log with source='sie_import' instead of a new table or sie_imports.migration_documentation: every reader (verifikat page, Rättad marker, behandlingshistorik, full archive) already renders that table, and the ledger stays #TRANS-only (SIE 4B rule: #RTRANS is twinned by an identical #TRANS, so summing all three double-counts). sie_import_id travels inside each payload entry rather than as a fifth RPC parameter, so the (uuid,uuid,uuid,jsonb) signature, its grants and its statement_timeout stay put and PostgREST sees no overload. Import history rows survive undo/replace like every other log row (no FK on purpose); a re-import writes fresh rows against fresh entry ids, the old ones only appear in behandlingshistoriken as history of the undone import.
[2026-09-09] Resultatrapport and dimension-pnl list account 8999 as a normal class-8 row instead of filtering it out (#2455): our own bokslut verifikat never posts 8999 (it zeroes each P&L account straight against 2099), so the only 8999 balances are manual or SIE-imported omföringar, and hiding those made an account-level report disagree with huvudboken. "Beräknat resultat" now reads zero after such an omföring, the Fortnox/Visma convention. Resultaträkning keeps excluding 8999 because ÅRL's uppställningsform has no such line. This supersedes the 2026-07-29 line that kept Resultatrapport on the same profit as Resultaträkning: they still share 'exclude-all-year-end', and now differ by exactly a booked 8999, pinned in cross-surface-agreement.test.ts. No MCP or v1 surface is built on generateResultatrapport (the income-statement tool and v1 route carry the word "resultatrapport" but serve Resultaträkning), so an agent asked for resultatrapporten still answers the computed figure: follow-up issue filed. Rejected the bigger version (Stage 2 of #1051, 'exclude-final'): with our closing entry shape it would zero every row of a closed year, and it is a separate decision.
[2026-09-09] SIE account creation writes each chunk as an ignore-duplicates upsert (ON CONFLICT (company_id, account_number) DO NOTHING, returning the landed rows) instead of a plain INSERT whose duplicate-key error was swallowed: under PostgREST one request is one transaction, so a single concurrent duplicate rolled back the whole statement while the caller counted it as created and moved on with accounts missing. The conflict clause makes the race a skipped row, `created` counts exactly what landed, and the "duplicate" string special-case is gone. Raised by CodeRabbit and the compliance review on PR #2451 (count accuracy under BFNAR 2013:2 p. 9.16); the audit_log trigger stays the per-row record.
[2026-09-10] Onboarding act two (#2438) hosts its own import surface instead of the SIE wizard, the migration workspace and the bank panel: same routes (sie/parse, sie/execute, arcim-migration/*, enable-banking/connect+accounts, skatteverket/authorize), fewer steps. Dropped on purpose: the Kontomappning page (unmapped accounts are created from the file via sie/create-accounts and mapped to themselves), the VAT review gate (momskoder for imported revenue accounts are set on the genomlysning or later in the chart), the voucher-series question (company default), the confirm modal and the underlag option (locked off; imported verifikat are marked no-doc-required on the SIE path). Nothing is ticked by default in the bank account picker and the fetch button exists only once an account is picked: nothing is fetched by accident. Ended imported years are klarmarkerade after import (reversible under Räkenskapsår). The bank callback keeps its /settings/banking landing; the first-session gate rewrite carries it into the act, so the callback is untouched.
[2026-09-10] Kollektivavtal semesterlön percentage (#2477) is a nullable per-employee column (employees.vacation_pay_rate, 0.135 = 13.5 %) resolved by one helper (lib/salary/vacation-pay-rate.ts) instead of a company-level setting: every other vacation setting (rule, days, semestertillägg) already lives on the employee row, the settings page points there, and a company with both LO and tjänstemannaavtal needs it per person anyway; a company default plus override would be a second state with the same result. The request arrived as "add 13.5 %"; the mechanism was the statutory rate hardcoded in six places (engine accrual, semesterersättning payout, calculateVacationAccrual, absence valuation, year-close dayValueSek, v1 vacation-balance inline copy), so the fix is one definition, and the v1 route now calls dayValueSek instead of carrying its own copy. The rate applies only under procentregeln and semesterersättning; sammalöneregeln keeps semestertillagg_rate. The statutory rate for the employee's entitlement is always the floor (max(CBA, statutory), so 13,5 % on a 30-day employee accrues 14,4 %): Semesterlagen 2 a § only lets a kollektivavtal improve on 16 b §; the first cut treated the CBA rate as absolute and the skeptic plus the compliance review on PR #2480 both flagged the 30-day under-payment. Enforced in the resolver, not the CHECK, because the DB bound cannot see the entitlement. Left alone on purpose: the statutory step (14.4 % only at 30 days, not 0.48 pp per extra day) is a separate decision.
[2026-09-10] Import notices get a severity tier (info | notice | action) and one renderer (#2461) instead of deleting warnings: import behaviour (renames, derived IB, 2099 adjustments) is behandlingshistorik under BFNAR 2013:2 and stays recorded in warnings/import_documentation, only its screen presence changes (info behind a tooltip, notices folded, one ochre action sentence per design convention 6). The structured list rides beside the legacy warnings: string[] rather than replacing it, because v1 API, MCP tools and stored documentation read the strings; the UI prefers notices and wraps unmigrated strings as legacy notices. lib/notices (the Hem slot) was not reused as the model: its ids are dismissal keys for company-wide states with reaping, not per-run import facts.
[2026-09-10] #2469 migration per-step requests instead of a background job with a progress table: the route already accepted per-step flags and dedupe makes reruns idempotent, so the wizard sequencing one request per step gives each step its own 300 s with no new schema or cron; revisit only if a single invoice step still exceeds the ceiling after the fiscal-year scope filter.
[2026-09-10] SIE import RPC (#2472) rewritten as one set-based statement (CTE chain: reserve, headers as posted, lines, history) with whole-file atomicity kept, instead of the proposed Stage B chunking with per-chunk transactions and dedupe: 5,000 vouchers on staging went 27.9 s to 8.3 s, under the 30 s bar set for building Stage B, and chunking would change the atomicity that sie-import-atomic.pg.test.ts and the unit suite pin. No temp table: header ids are matched back to their payload ordinal by (series, voucher_number), unique within the call because each series gets a contiguous block. Headers insert directly as posted so check_balance_on_posted_insert verifies once at commit and the draft-to-posted UPDATE (and its second audit_log row per voucher) is gone. Remaining floor is the per-row triggers on journal_entry_lines (audit, immutability, writer-role, lock date); Stage B stays deferred until a real file exceeds ~30 s.
[2026-09-10] Set-based SIE import (#2472) drops the COMMITTED_AT_OVERRIDE audit row that service-role imports (v1 API key, MCP pending operation) used to get per voucher: the old draft-to-posted UPDATE carried committed_at = now(), so set_committed_at v3 (20260810121000) logged an override whose preset time equalled the wall clock, one noise row per imported voucher (3,678 in prod over 60 days). Behandlingshistoriken still shows the bokföringspost from journal_entries.committed_at, which the new body sets to the same now(). A real preset (seeding flows backdating history) still goes through the engine's UPDATE path and is still logged.
[2026-09-10] Migration result: 'Importerad med varning' label dropped, the untransferred-result (8999) fact is one section-level AttnLine and per-year status is Importerad / Delvis importerad / Misslyckades only: the fact is company scoped, not year status, and painting correct behaviour (renames, derived IB) ochre made users read a good import as broken (#2462). Renames and the continuation IB-skip moved from warnings to result.accountsRenamed / details.openingBalanceSkipped, still recorded for BFNAR 2013:2; severity codes are phase 2 (#2461).
[2026-09-10] BankID #2471: the completed identification is sealed (AES-256-GCM) into the signed flow cookie the first time /poll sees it, and /poll, the probe, /complete and /link read it from there; TIC hands a completed result out at most twice, so a third fetch (the iPhone reload-probe-Fortsätt-collect path) can never succeed. Rejected: retrying collect (cannot work), a bankid_session_results table (migration, pg test, cleanup and a service-role write from a skipAuth route for state the cookie already carries with the same privilege), signing without encrypting (the signup path needs the raw personnummer and the cookie payload is plain base64).
[2026-09-10] Peppol registration errors (#2483): the hosted connector code is kept as a first-class field (PeppolTransportError.code, peppol_registrations.last_error_code) and translated through the error registry; retryable vs permanent is decided from the transport's retryable flag plus a fixed set of hosted verdict codes, not by parsing the prose. Rejected: translating the composed English string with regexes (the code already exists on the wire and the string is built for logs). The stale-pending window (5 min) is one definition in lib/invoices/peppol-registration.ts, so GET reports stale_pending rather than the page re-deriving it from updated_at; a stale pending row is retired as failed before a new attempt because the live-row unique index would otherwise block the insert.
[2026-09-10] Peppol registration errors (#2483, skeptic round): peppol_registrations.last_error_code stores the verdict (PEPPOL_REGISTRATION_REJECTED / _FAILED) unless the transport code is a registry-known verdict of its own; CONNECTOR_UPSTREAM_ERROR and HTTP_<n> are never stored because they wrap whatever the access point said (a permanent Qvalia refusal arrives as UPSTREAM_ERROR with retryable=false), so storing the wrapper would show retry advice and a retry button on a refusal. The transport code still travels in the response details and the log. can_retry is decided server-side next to the registry; the UI never guesses from the code. A bare HTTP_404 keeps the row live (every hosted 404 is a CONNECTOR_NOT_OWNED envelope; a bare one means the connector URL missed the route).
[2026-09-10] Trial balance aggregation moved into SQL (#2470, get_trial_balance_aggregates, migration 20260910163731): one plpgsql RPC returns per-account sums in a 'period' and a 'rollforward' bucket under the three ClosingEntryMode predicates, ported verbatim from the two fetchEntryLines passes (include and exclude-all-year-end from get_kpi_report_aggregates, exclude-final from get_vat_declaration_totals); generateTrialBalance keeps its signature, the period/accounts/OB reads, the fail-closed guard and the row-building tail. plpgsql over LANGUAGE sql because the fail-closed guard and the mode check raise. The guard is duplicated in JS on purpose: the unit suite asserts no journal read is issued for a closed period without closing_entry_id, and the JS already holds the period row. Opening balances stay in getOpeningBalances (unchanged diff surface; folding them in is the follow-up's call). The chunked JS path stays behind REPORTS_TB_RPC=off for one release, pinned by the pre-existing unit suites, and is deleted with the flag when the other fetchEntryLines aggregators move to the RPC. Parity (scripts/parity-trial-balance-rpc.ts, zero öre diffs across every company and period; the only paired error that counts as parity is the exclude-final fail-closed guard, any other error on either path is a diff) runs on staging before merge and on prod read-only right after the migration lands there: a pre-merge prod run would need either a prod Postgres connection string (not in any env file) or DDL on prod, both outside what agents may do.
[2026-09-11] Image CVEs (#2490): exclude the Docker runner stage from the buildx cache instead of bumping the base digest again: the digest bump (#2071) fixes one advisory and re-breaks on the next, the cache filter re-runs apk upgrade on every build for seconds of cost. mailparser pinned to 3.9.20, the last release on nodemailer 9.x, so its nested copy dedupes with the pinned 9.1.1 rather than pulling a second major.
[2026-09-11] Multi-user seat gate switched OFF by default instead of only hiding its banner (#2494): a trial that ends must change nothing for the people in a company, and the 20-day grace countdown ("pausas om 17 dagar") read as an account being paused or deleted. isMultiUserEnforced now returns false on hosted unless MULTI_USER_SEAT_GATE=true; the state model, RPCs and every downstream dormancy check stay intact so one env flip re-arms it. Deleted the user-facing surfaces that can no longer render (grace banner, /paused page, grace reminder cron and mails, their message keys). Rejected: banner-only removal (the freeze would still land silently on day 20), full removal of the seat model (larger diff across middleware, api-v1 and MCP auth paths for the same visible result). The post-trial dialog is reframed as an invitation ("Vill du fortsätta använda Accounted med alla kopplingar och förmåner?") and the nav pill drops "Provperioden är slut"; the trial countdown pill stays because it only states what the trial is.
[2026-09-10] Peppol inbound reprocessing (#2485): the pass selects `routed` rows too, not only received/unrouted/failed as the card listed, because a document held back for lack of XML is left `routed` with last_error 'awaiting xml' and would otherwise never be delivered; rows whose last_error starts with 'terminal:' are excluded in the query (a LIKE filter) as well as skipped in code, since skipping alone would let 25 terminal rows starve every later pass. No JSON-only rendition exists in peppol-inbox-delivery.ts, so a document whose XML is terminally unavailable upstream stays pending and is counted as terminal rather than filed without its underlag. Terminal rows are also skipped by the listing sync (one definition in processInboundPeppolRow) so the two passes cannot disagree. No migration: the existing partial status index covers the predicate at these volumes.
[2026-09-10] Peppol inbound reprocessing (#2485, skeptic round): the listing sync archives oldest first and stops the document type at the first transient archive failure, because the receivedAfter cursor is derived from the newest archived received_at and must never point past a document that is not in the archive; a deterministic insert failure (CHECK / NOT NULL / bad value, Postgres 22xxx and 23502/23514) is recorded as a terminal stub row under the same provider id (ubl_json {}) so it pages once and stops blocking the cursor. A null XML answer is a miss, terminal after 3 (count kept in last_error, cleared when the XML arrives); unreadable payloads and missing recipient endpoints are terminal on sight. The cron pages once per run only for newly terminal documents (provider ids listed) and real failures; transport errors, misses under budget and held documents (awaiting XML, awaiting an owner member) are warned and retried. A held or terminal document has no user-facing surface yet (no inbox item without XML by choice; peppol_inbound_documents has no UI): showing the user an 'underlag saknas' item is a founder call for Emil.
[2026-09-10] Peppol inbound reprocessing (#2485, PR #2492 round): the archive stamps processed_at at insert so the pass predicate is a plain `lt` plus one literal `or` (the column guard cannot read a filter built from a template literal, and the ceiling stays at 404); the recipient resolver reads the exact digits-only identifier first and only then scans registrations in code, paginated with fetchAllRows, for rows stored with formatting; the listing cursor is one second before the newest archived received_at so a same-second sibling listed next run is not skipped (the unique key absorbs the overlap); the cron's single alert covers listing-sync failures as well as reprocess failures and newly terminal documents.
[2026-09-10] Template catalog gap fill: 22 templates chosen from twelve months of prod bank bookings (accounts used by 3+ companies with no template), not from a competitor's catalog; the keyword matcher loses precision with near-duplicates. Skipped on purpose: 1580 payment-service clearing (not in the BAS reference), 2012 EF F-skatt (the skattekonto flow books it), non-EU goods import (needs the tullräkning, not the supplier payment). Hyrbil keywords and MCC 7512 moved from travel_transport and vehicle_leasing to the new vehicle_rental (5820).
[2026-09-10] Account erasure completed (branch fix/account-erasure-gdpr): one public.erase_user_personal_data(uuid) holds all erasure, called by anonymize_user_account after its guards and by the migration's repair pass over every existing tombstone, instead of a second copy of the statements for repair (20260803090000 duplicated its WhatsApp block). Erasure stays an explicit list guarded by a pg ratchet over every public FK to auth.users (tests/pg/account-erasure.pg.test.ts: erased or retained) rather than a catalog loop that deletes from every ON DELETE CASCADE table: the migration template puts `user_id ... ON DELETE CASCADE` on company bookkeeping tables, so a generic loop would delete räkenskapsinformation the first time a new table went unlisted, while under the ratchet an unlisted table fails CI.
[2026-09-10] The tombstone's auth.users.email is now cleared at deletion, SUPERSEDING the re-signup block (route comment; 2026-07-24 BankID signup line): the published privacy policy keeps account data at most 30 days after a deletion request, and blocking re-registration is a product preference rather than a retention basis. Consequence: a deleted address can register a new, empty account. Founder alternative if the block matters: keep the address 30 days and clear it with a purge job.
[2026-09-10] Bank and mail connections of an erased user are revoked in place (status 'revoked'; session, tokens and accounts_data shredded), not deleted: transactions, cash_accounts and imported underlag reference the rows, and both readers (bank sync cron, mail search) select status = 'active' only. In a migration-reset source company, block_migration_reset_source_mutation lets exactly that revoke through, once (the rest of the row must be identical) instead of erasure skipping those rows: a consent is not an accounting record, and skipping would leave its credentials in place (raised in review on #2495). The route's auth.admin.signOut(user.id, 'global') is removed: it takes a JWT and returned an unread error, so it never ended a session; the RPC deletes sessions and refresh tokens instead.
[2026-09-11] Dead-file sweep (#2510): deleted only zero-reference items; kept data/tax-tables/2026 (input for scripts/import-tax-tables.ts), the two example extensions (cited by docs and the create-extension skill), InboxCustomDomainDialog (gated off on purpose), the push-notifications workspace (extension enabled self-hosted), bolagsverket extension and three comment-referenced lib modules (unwired but not proven dead). Historical comments that name a deleted script were left as-is: they describe what ran, not what to run.
[2026-09-11] Dead-file sweep (#2510) skeptic round: six scripts restored because they are pending or live procedures, not history: repair-stranded-categorized-transactions.ts (#2057 prod run still waits for founder go), backfill-document-storage-paths.ts (Phase B of migration 20260726092000, 16k legacy objects not yet re-homed), repair-result-appropriation.ts (named by year-end-service.ts as the recovery path), repair-customer-personal-number-in-org-number.ts, backfill-has-password.ts, migrate-sie-files-to-company-paths.ts (no completion record found). Rule applied: any doubt means keep.
[2026-09-11] sru_code is derived from the INK2R mapping (lib/reports/ink2/account-mappings.ts), not from a separate range table: the chart, the seed, SIE #SRU and the filed INK2R must agree, and the 20240101000021 table (NE-style 73xx codes, 12xx on 7202, 25xx-29xx on 7231) had 132k prod rows on codes that are not INK2R fields. The backfill only touches rows still holding the legacy value; hand-set codes stay.
[2026-09-11] TIC org-number lookup ranks the related hits (active before ceased, then latest registrationDate) instead of taking Typesense's first hit: a person with two enskilda firmor otherwise gets the one closed in 2012 written into the new company.
[2026-09-11] Year-end sequencing (feedback seq 392722): a pre-locked period is refused by a readiness blocker (PERIOD_LOCKED, kind period_locked) and by gnubok_run_year_end at stage time, not tolerated by silently unlocking as the reporter proposed: a lock a human took stays meaningful, and executeYearEndClosing already locks the period itself after posting the closing entry. period_locked is summarized under "period-state" in the readiness description rather than named: tools/list sits 6 tokens under its 60 500 ceiling and the blocker message carries its own remediation. The skill, the lock_period/run_year_end next-hints and the server instructions now all describe the one flow the service implements (readiness, run_year_end on the open period, verify) instead of the three-step lock, year-end, close prose that predated the service.
[2026-09-11] "Period is already closed" / "already locked" keep their plain-string throw sites (server.ts and period-service.ts) and are mapped in inferCode plus the Swedish pattern map, rather than converting every site to a coded error: one mapping covers the six sites and the registry entry carries the remediation. PERIOD_ALREADY_CLOSED is a new code because TARGET_PERIOD_CLOSED is date-addressed ("the period covering this date") and FX_PERIOD_CLOSED is revaluation-specific.
[2026-09-11] gnubok_book_skattekonto_row(s) descriptions name only the ränta/avgift/prelskatt/moms rules (not the bank in-/utbetalning and AGI rules): the 280-char description budget applies to search-only tools too, and the keywords array (free, never serialized) carries the rest of the searchable vocabulary.
[2026-09-11] MCP vat_declaration_status (feedback seq 330091): the Skatteverket redovisningsperiod for helårsmoms is now resolved by ONE helper, resolveRedovisningsperiod in the skatteverket extension (used by buildMomsuppgift, the HTTP status service and the MCP status tool), instead of delegating the MCP tool to fetchVatDeclarationStatus: that service uses the company-scoped read auth (#1673) and a `{ ok: false }` error contract, while the MCP tool authenticates as the calling user and throws registry-coded errors, so delegation would have widened auth as a side effect of a period fix. The reporter's alternative, accepting an explicit redovisningsperiod argument, was declined: it lets an agent poll or file under a period the books do not describe. resolvePeriodDates now returns `source`; vat_close_check exposes it as `period.source` (the outputSchema keeps `period` an open object, so tools/list cost is zero) and adds a `fiscal_year_not_found` blocker only when the yearly fallback fires for a company whose fiscal year does not start in January (calendar-FY companies with no fiscal_periods row are correct on the fallback).
[2026-09-11] create_supplier_invoice_from_inbox defaults a missing due date to invoice_date + suppliers.default_payment_terms (30 when unset) and labels it due_date_source: 'defaulted' instead of staging null or rejecting: supplier_invoices.due_date is NOT NULL, bank fees and kvitton carry no förfallodatum, and the human approver sees the label; a null total is rejected at staging (SI_CREATE_INVALID_INPUT, remediation gnubok_set_inbox_extracted_data) rather than derived from sum(lineItems), because the header amount is what gets credited on 2440 and must be confirmed against the underlag, not guessed.
[2026-09-11] Onboarding act one: an EF whose orgnr lookup returned a name no longer gets the verksamhetsnamn question (nextCompanyStep treats a lookup name as known for every form): the founder wants orgnr + Enter to be the whole step, the wizard's confirm-the-name beat read as a second step, and the name stays editable in Settings. An EF without lookup data still names its verksamhet.
[2026-09-13] AI connection state per client: the MCP OAuth token route now stores which built-in client minted a key (api_keys.client, from the redirect URI the allowlist already classifies) instead of adding an mcp_connections table or a per-client key name. The provider was derived at consent and discarded; one nullable column with no CHECK (a new provider must not 500 every fresh authorization) is the smallest state that lets the books act's Done step and Hem show Claude/ChatGPT/Grok as connected. Keys from before the column, Cursor, localhost and registered clients stay null and read as none of the three.
[2026-09-13] Hem after the books act: finishing the act (POST /api/onboarding/books/exit outcome done) stamps initial_setup_completed_at, so the getting-started checklist retires and Att göra opens first; a Kopplingar strip under it keeps the declined bank and Skatteverket connections visible (founder call: keep the strip, kill the checklist). The strip replaces the dismissible Skatteverket promo sentence (SkatteverketPromoCard deleted) so one surface offers both connections. The Att göra footer hands the FIRST row to a connected client through the client's ?q= new-chat link (claude.ai/new, chatgpt.com, grok.com) rather than copy-to-clipboard; rows a human must act on (staged operations, the two Betala transfers) are skipped when picking that row.
[2026-09-14] Books act, provider path: the door onward (Fortsätt till banken) now renders only when the import succeeded; a failed import gets Försök igen + Välj en annan källa instead. The manual SIE path already gated on canContinue; the Fortnox path showed the button on any end of the import phase, which is how a 0-of-2,786 import on staging (RPC execute grant missing, repaired 2026-09-13) reached Klart and Hem. Retry is safe by construction: failed sie_imports rows never block the overlap check and an existing opening balance is skipped.

[2026-09-11] SIE import backbone replaces the one-shot writer with database-owned leased jobs, bounded transactions and immutable batch receipts. A lost response is reconciled under the writer lock and fenced attempt number. Per-source and batch-position uniqueness enforce retry safety; a different prepared content hash is a named conflict. Whole-file visibility is intentionally replaced by partial visibility plus database holds and external-report read leases. Direct Postgres bypass and client-only workers were declined because neither solves durable recovery or idempotency.
[2026-09-11] SIE cutover keeps admission, execution pause and compact audit as separate switches. Admission-off drains existing jobs; worker-pause stops claims during investigation. The old import/period-delete RPCs are retired at the coordinated application/schema cutover, so an application-only git revert is not a safe rollback. New owned-source uniqueness ships first; the all-history constraint waits for individually reviewed legacy source-key collisions.
[2026-09-11] SIE undo and duplicate repair retain original vouchers and post bounded storno through the bookkeeping engine. Historical repair uses reviewed exact keep/reverse IDs, immutable full-content and dependent-record fingerprints, and per-chunk receipts rather than assigning guessed batch ownership or deleting a fiscal period. A stopped repair preserves completed corrections and cancels only untouched targets; ordinary import undo/replacement cannot revive it.
[2026-09-11] SIE compact audit is implemented as an opt-in candidate pinned in each job manifest, retaining source archives, mapping choices, exact entry receipts and processing-history rendering. Full audit remains default until separate accounting review and founder enablement. No legal-trigger shortcut or speculative index pruning ships; index removal requires a full observation window and rare-period query review.
[2026-09-11] Emil chose manual review instead of automatic adjacent-year opening-balance replacement during SIE backfill. Completion records the review atomically without writing next-year vouchers; source undo renews its token. Owner/admin acknowledgment is audited and stale confirmations fail without transport retries. Review remains advisory and separate from execution holds.
[2026-09-11] Emil delegated the durable-import reset choice to the most retention-preserving approach: refuse year deletion when durable imports or reviewed repairs exist, including after storno, and direct owners to the existing whole-company archive/start-fresh flow. Preserve that flow's confirmations and eligibility, extend its unfinished-import guard to durable undo, and use the full retained-source archive so vouchers and linked documents remain downloadable.
[2026-09-13] SIE review keeps read-only period observation locks shared while admission remains exclusive, validates parser errors before admission, normalizes new duplicate mapping inputs while preserving existing checkpoint positions, and binds archive reads to generated source identities including retained pre-tenant paths. These close concurrent-write, incomplete-import, post-write finalization and privileged-source-read failures without weakening accounting guards.
[2026-09-11] INK2R ranges (and therefore sru_code) follow the official BAS kopplingstabell checked in as lib/reports/ink2/official-ink2r-coupling.json and pinned by a test: bas.se INK2_P1_intervall-241119.xlsx is the newest coupling (checked 2026-09-11; BAS 2026 v1.1 has no SRU column) and the engine's own table deviated on real accounts (24xx shifted a decade, 213x, 1520-1589, 138x, 128x, 82xx, nedskrivningar, 49xx). Plus/minus rows now file a negative net in the minus-box field (INK2R_SIGN_TWINS) instead of a negative amount. Extensions beyond the file (2010-2079, 48xx, 1670, 2473, 2860-2873, 46xx as handelsvaror) are listed in the test.
[2026-09-11] Large sru_code backfills run inside BEGIN/SET LOCAL gnubok.sandbox_cleanup/COMMIT so write_audit_log skips the per-row jsonb audit rows: sru_code is a tax-form label, not bookkeeping, and the 2026-09-11 backfill (330k rows) would otherwise have written ~0.5 GB of audit rows and stalled the Supabase runner.
[2026-09-11] GET /api/v1/.../accounts orders by account_number instead of documenting its sort_order order: account_number IS the BAS sequence (a 5-digit sub-account like 19301 lands right after 1930), while chart_of_accounts.sort_order is 0 on every seeded system account (61k prod rows) and parseInt(number) elsewhere, so the old order put the seeded block first. Fixing the order kept the documented contract; documenting the wart would have kept a useless one. sort_order stays in the response (removing a field breaks clients) and the data is not backfilled.
[2026-09-11] Account lists (list_company_accounts RPC, its paged fallback, MCP gnubok_list_accounts) order by account_number instead of backfilling chart_of_accounts.sort_order: account_number is already the BAS sequence and unique per company, while sort_order is 0 on every seeded account and written inconsistently by the insert paths. A backfill would keep a second source of truth nobody maintains; the column stays on the row because API clients read it.
[2026-09-11] The NULL-SRU backfill for charts seeded 2026-03-31..06-12 (#2517) writes the current seed_chart_of_accounts() literals per account number, not the INK2R range table: the goal is a chart identical to one seeded today, and none of those codes lies in a range #2520 moves, so it is correct whichever of the two runs first. System rows with NULL only (user codes and user accounts untouched; EF equity and förening accounts stay NULL on purpose); audited (one audit_log row per account, actor_type 'system', label = the migration) unlike the two ~330k-row SRU backfills of 2026-09-11: at 21,840 rows the behandlingshistorik trail (BFNAR 2013:2 p. 9.16) costs a few tens of MB, so size no longer justifies skipping it.
[2026-09-11] create_supplier_invoice_from_inbox under reverse charge (feedback seq 366701): the payable (subtotal, total, remaining_amount) is the sum of the line nets and vat_amount is 0, both at staging and again in the executor, instead of the extraction's gross: the registration entry credits 2440 with the line nets (the 2614/2645 pair nets to zero), so a gross reskontra could never be settled against the GL. VAT a seller charged on a reverse-charge invoice is not deductible ingående moms and is surfaced in the preview (payable_recomputed + warning), not booked; whether to pay it anyway or ask for a corrected invoice is the buyer's call against the underlag. The reporter's alternative, a separate payable_amount next to the gross total, was declined: two totals leave the wrong one in the reskontra.
[2026-09-11] MCP briefing + search_tools report callability instead of promoting search-only writes (feedback seq 372962, 335021, 381082, 414922, 371965): recommended_tools entries carry callable/blocked_by/note (scope from the injected __keyScopes marker, catalog from the search-only WRITE tier) and search hits carry callable_via; nothing is dropped and no tool changes catalog tier, because tools/list sits single-digit tokens under its 60 500 ceiling and a promotion or a write bridge with honest annotations is a separate founder decision. A missing __keyScopes marker fails closed (no scopes) in the briefing, same contract as search_tools, so a direct execute() never vouches for a scoped tool. isDefaultCatalogTool moved to tool-reach.ts (re-exported from server.ts) so recommended-tools.ts can share the classification without an import cycle.
[2026-09-11] Visma unpaid invoices imported as paid (feedback seq 409660/409630/398500, company 5208b894): the paid flag and the balance are two independent signals and the mapper combined them with OR, so a present-but-zero RemainingAmount overrode an explicit PaymentStatus = Unpaid. Fixed by a combination rule, not by re-auditing the enum table (#1462 already did that): PaymentStatusDto carries `source: 'enum' | 'balance'`, the Visma mapper reports the open balance as `remaining > 0 ? remaining : total` whenever the enum says unsettled, and entity-mapper lets the balance declare a settlement only when no enum spoke. Optional field, so the five other provider mappers keep their balance-derived semantics (a residual öre still resolves to paid) until they opt in. Supplier credit notes now carry paid_amount = remaining_amount = 0, as the customer side already did.
[2026-09-11] Duplicate customers/suppliers from a re-run step (987 + 9 rows in company 5208b894): mitigated in code by re-reading the register delta (created_at > snapshot, 30 s lookback) right before each chunk insert and by an in-flight lock on the wizard's submit, instead of a migration. The durable fix is a unique index on customers/suppliers (company_id, org_number); suppliers deliberately have no unique constraint yet (#840 design, prod holds legitimate same-org rows), so that index is a founder call and not taken here.
[2026-09-11] Dropped invoice_inbox_items_journal_entry_unique (migration 20260911120500) instead of making gnubok_list_unmatched_documents / gnubok_list_inbox_items read document_attachments.journal_entry_id as the reporters proposed (feedback seq 389343, 395894, 395931, 366701): the UNIQUE modelled a 1:1 the product never had (an invoice plus its payment confirmation on one verifikat), never guarded the same-row double commit it was added for (a cross-row UNIQUE cannot see two UPDATEs of one row; the CAS predicates on the stamping UPDATEs do), and the link_document_to_voucher stamp swallowed its 23505 silently. Deriving "processed" from the inbox row's own link columns keeps one source of truth instead of two predicates that can disagree; the stamp now logs a warning on zero matched rows instead of tolerating a unique violation.
