[2026-09-11] Salary journal preview (feedback seq 384229): the preview route now renders buildSalaryRunEntryLines, the same pure builder createSalaryRunEntries posts, and every booking surface (book-run.ts, the v1 book route) maps rows through one salaryRunDataFromRows, instead of patching the missing benefit-type skip into the preview's own loop. The preview had drifted on four rules (benefit lines, öresavrundning, kostnadsersättning, the tax override) and the v1 route was a third copy of the roster mapping; a rule now lives in one place or nowhere. The response carries balanced/difference per entry and the review card shows a red line when false: the DB trigger refuses an unbalanced voucher at booking, the preview never had a check. A zero-avgifter run still previews no avgifter entry (nollkörning parity), decided by the lines being all-zero rather than by a preview-side recomputation.
[2026-09-11] IB resync series (feedback seq 345150): resyncNextPeriodOpeningBalance reads the replaced IB voucher's voucher_series and passes it to replaceOpeningBalanceEntry instead of letting the engine resolve the company default: commit_opening_balance_replacement stornos the old entry in ITS series (COALESCE(old.voucher_series, 'A')) and no migration changes that here, so the only way to keep storno and replacement together is to follow the old entry. A null series maps to 'A' to mirror the RPC's fallback; a failed read throws rather than guessing. Alternative rejected: resolving both from settings would need the RPC to take the storno series too (a migration), for the same visible result.
[2026-09-11] K2 sweep and 8990-8999 (feedback seq 345150): the unmapped-account sweep now skips årets resultat accounts and pushes one informational line per account into the same warnings array ("ingår inte i K2-mappningen: årets resultat beräknas från resultaträkningens summa. Ingen åtgärd behövs."), rather than staying silent or adding a separate notes channel to K2MappingResult: warnings is the only surface the ÅR preview renders, and a reader who sees 8999 with a balance deserves to be told why it is absent from the mapping. Mapping 8999 to a concept was rejected: årets resultat is derived from the RR sum and a mapped 8999 would double it.
[2026-09-11] Medelantal anställda missing vs zero (feedback seq 366434): one resolveMedelantalNote in build-data.ts serves the K2 and K3 noter builders (the sentence lived twice) and decides "saknas" from period activity on 7000-7399 in the tbFullRows the builders already receive (no new query; period_debit/period_credit because with the closing entry included every 7xxx nets to zero after bokslut). An explicit override, 0 included, is the user's statement and keeps "inte haft några anställda" with no warning: hasMedelantalOverride is exported from lib/salary/medelantal.ts so the resolver and the note cannot disagree on what "set" means. 7400-7699 (pension, avgifter, övriga personalkostnader) are excluded from the signal on purpose: a company can carry those without a single anställd. The note and warning are only produced where the note is produced today (AB, or medelantal > 0), so enskild firma is not nagged about a note it does not file.
[2026-09-11] bank_connection.sync_failed (feedback seq 340107): one classifier + emitter (extensions/general/enable-banking/lib/sync-failure-event.ts) called once at the top of each sync catch (agent trigger, cron route, manual route) with the post-handling status, instead of a log line per branch or an emit inside each branch: three paths already had three copies of the branch ladder, and a per-branch emit would be a fourth thing to keep aligned. The payload carries userId (event_log stores it in its own column; the cron uses the connection owner) and a trigger field beyond the requested shape, since "which path failed" was the first question on 2026-09-03. message is the internal error text capped at 500 chars; the ConnectorSyncError body stays out (it can carry transaction and personal data), the Enable Banking envelope only contributes its code. The emit never throws (a bus failure must not change the sync outcome), and eventBus.emit already settles handler errors. The inline initial backfill after the callback (index.ts, syncPromise.catch(() => {})) is left alone: it swallows on purpose behind a timeout policy and is not a sync users retry.
[2026-09-11] suggestVatTreatment matches the union through two shared constants (UNION = /\b(?:eu|eg)\b/, OUTSIDE_UNION = /utanför\s+(?:eu|eg)\b/) instead of adding "eg" to each of the six literal regexes that spelled it out (#2525): the term list being duplicated per branch is the mechanism, not a missing word, and it is why the class 3 branch sent EG labels to momsfri ruta 42 while the class 4-6 branch dropped them from the declaration entirely. "EG" (Europeiska gemenskapen) is the pre-Lisbon name and one chart carries both spellings, since accounts added later picked up current BAS names while older ones were never renamed. OUTSIDE_UNION gained a trailing \b in the same edit, which also stops "utanför Europa" from reading as an export and being zero-rated. Verified against the full BAS 2026 chart: zero of 1286 accounts change suggestion, and no BAS name matches "eg" that did not already match "eu".
[2026-09-11] The trepartshandel row from #2525 (3057 "Treparts försäljn varor till EG 25%") is knowingly left suggesting reverse_charge_eu_goods/ruta 35 when the correct box is 38: there is no trepartshandel value in ACCOUNT_VAT_TREATMENTS and the column is guarded by chart_of_accounts_default_vat_treatment_check, so a correct suggestion needs a new treatment plus a migration, a pg-real test and UI/i18n, which is a feature rather than a term fix. Ruta 35 was preferred over a null suggestion (which would drop the amount from the declaration) and over the current ruta 05 (which asserts Swedish output VAT on an intermediary sale); class 3 rows always sit behind the import review gate, so the row is shown before anything persists.
[2026-09-11] The #2525 regression fixture (lib/import/__tests__/fixtures/eg-eu-bas97.se) is committed as real CP437 bytes marked `binary` in .gitattributes, not as UTF-8: the decode is part of what the test asserts, since suggestions are computed from the decoded label and a matcher that is correct on a UTF-8 string still produces the wrong momskod if decodeBuffer hands it mojibake ("utanför" arriving broken would stop OUTSIDE_UNION matching and send 3045/3055 to the intra-union branch). A UTF-8 fixture cannot catch that class. The accompanying test runs the whole wizard chain (detectEncoding, decodeBuffer, parseSIEFile, suggestMappings, enrichAccountMappingsWithVat) rather than the matcher alone, because the enrichment is client-side and a green unit suite says nothing about the flow: verified by reverting the fix, where 11 of its 13 assertions fail with the reported symptom. The fixture is anonymised (invented company, Luhn-valid test orgnr, invented amounts); only the EU-BAS 97 account labels are real, and those are already published in the issue.
[2026-09-12] The "momskoder att granska" filter keeps rows edited through the two selects visible via a component-local editedThisStep set (#2537), rather than widening the filter to `requiresVatTreatmentReview` alone or snapshotting a worklist when the filter is selected: each row carries a momskod AND a separate sats, changing either marks the row reviewed, and picking a treatment assigns a default rate (0.25 for reverse charge on class 4-6), so the row dropped out of the list still carrying the wrong rate ("Inköp varor 12% EG" became 25% and vanished). The two predicates now live in lib/import/account-vat-treatment.ts as needsVatTreatmentReview and isInVatReviewList, because the same expression was duplicated between the list filter (AccountMappingStep.tsx:117) and the chip counter (:163) and that duplication is what let them mean different things; the counter keeps the narrow predicate so it still counts down. The per-row confirm button and "Bekräfta alla föreslagna" deliberately do not feed the set: they mean "done with this row". Setting isOverride was rejected outright: it means the account was remapped to a different target, and overloading it would misreport the mapping and risk persisting a mapping override the user never made.
[2026-09-12] Follow-up on #2537 found FOUR more inline copies of `requiresVatTreatmentReview && !vatTreatmentReviewed` beyond the two first consolidated (AccountMappingStep default-filter and per-row confirm gate, import/page.tsx show-the-step and block-continue gates, ArcimMigrationWorkspace auto-skip, and applyVatTreatmentReviewAll), and the confirm-button copy is what made the first fix visibly wrong: the row was held in the list while its confirm control vanished, so it read as unfinished with no way to finish it. All six now call needsVatTreatmentReview, except the confirm button which uses isInVatReviewList so control and row cannot drift apart again. The confirm handler also releases the row from editedThisStep: without that the button is inert on an edited row, since the sticky set keeps the row visible no matter what the button does. That combination (edit keeps the row, confirm releases it) was verified in the running UI, not only in tests; the release itself lives in component state and is outside the repo's test scope.
[2026-09-12] The import mapping table's Konfidens column became Matchning and now renders from the mapping itself rather than from a thresholded score (#2539): suggestMappings emits exactly three confidence values (1.0 exact BAS match, 0.7 valid BAS-range self-map, 0 no match), so the badge re-derived by inequality a three-valued enum that matchType already carried, and the 0.7 band displayed "Trolig" on identity mappings (4057 to 4057, created at import), inventing a doubt that does not exist for 11 of the 12 accounts in the #2527 fixture and for most real charts. The "Osäker" branch was unreachable: the only zero-confidence case carries an empty targetAccount and the badge was guarded on the target being present. Since the only non-identity mappings in the system are the single group-header redirect (2640 to 2641) and a target the user picked, the column now labels all four states positively (BAS-konto, Från filen, Flyttad, Manuell) rather than leaving the majority blank: an empty cell in a migration reads as "nothing known" rather than "this is fine". "Från filen" was chosen over "eget konto" deliberately, because bas_range covers BOTH a source system's invention (a Fortnox 4599) AND a standard sub-account absent from our 1286-entry reference (1241 Personbilar is verifiably not in it); claiming the account is the company's own would assert what the mapper cannot know, repeating the exact mistake "Trolig" made. "Från filen" says only what is true: the number is kept and the name and type come from the upload, which the Målkonto column corroborates by showing the BAS name on BAS-konto rows and the file's name on the others. Rejected: renaming only the 0.7 band, which removes the false claim but keeps a fake numeric scale and the discarded matchType; and hiding the column inside the vat_review filter (the original suggestion in #2538), which treats the symptom. The confidence field itself stays: it is persisted in sie_account_mappings and feeds averageConfidence. The badge strings moved to messages/sv.json and en.json; the import mapping UI is not on the "stays Swedish" list in .claude/rules/i18n.md, so the previous hardcoded literals were a gap rather than an exemption.
[2026-09-12] The "osäkra" filter chip and its low_confidence FilterType were removed from the import mapping step (#2539): the predicate was `targetAccount && confidence < 0.7`, and the only zero-confidence rows are precisely those with an empty targetAccount, so the chip could never be anything but 0 and the filter could never match a row. It read "0 osäkra" on every import. The intro prose lost its referent at the same time and now says "granska de markerade nedan" instead of "de osäkra", since rows needing attention are marked by the warning border on the momskod select rather than by a confidence band. Deliberately NOT removed: the wider lowConfidence surface in SIEPreviewStep, import/page.tsx (which holds a third copy of the 0.7 threshold), account-mapper's stats (0.5) and types.ts. That is a larger cleanup across the preview step's warnings and does not belong in a fix to one column; it is the same duplicated-threshold shape as #2537 and worth its own pass.
[2026-09-12] Invoice type toggles (offert/proforma/återkommande/självfaktura) are four separate company_settings booleans, default true, hiding entry points only: faktura is not toggleable (it is the product) and följesedel is left out (no entry point of its own, only the editor picker); existing documents of a hidden kind stay under Alla and the API/MCP ignore the flags, mirroring sales_orders_enabled.
[2026-09-12] Offerter is its own page (/quotes) and nav row above Kundfakturor instead of a view inside the invoice list: a quote is not an invoice (founder direction). The list page is reused through a variant context rather than copied, quotes leave every invoice view, and quotes_enabled only hides the nav row: the page, the rows and the APIs stay.
[2026-09-13] The decision lines of open PRs #2519 #2521 #2522 #2523 #2524 #2527 #2538 #2541 #2542 are landed on main ahead of the PRs and the branches are made DECISIONS.md-neutral: GitHub has no union merge driver, so nine branches that each append to the end of DECISIONS.md flip every other one to CONFLICTING after each merge, one full CI wave per merge; with the lines already on main the branches stay mergeable and one CI wave covers the batch.
[2026-09-13] SIE reports share observer locks; leases still exclude imports. Validate annual snapshots before persistence. Sandbox cleanup requires trusted context and exact company classification. Real-company retention stays strict; see docs/operations/sie-import-backbone.md.
[2026-09-13] Archived DECISIONS.md entries dated before 2026-08-01 to git history (c36bc125a) and capped new entries at 300 characters: at 1.1 MB the log was too large to check before re-litigating, and long entries had turned into PR bodies.
[2026-09-13] Removed the create-ticket, docker-patterns, mcp-builder and erp-api-route skills plus the vendored .agents Vercel skills (founder call, overrides the 07-29 keep of web-design-guidelines): generic or stale, and erp-api-route taught the forbidden getUser() route.
[2026-09-13] Cite DECISIONS entries by date, never by line number: union merges shift lines, and eight code comments citing DECISIONS.md:632 pointed at an unrelated Google Drive entry.
[2026-09-13] Removed shell v1 (phase 9b of #2390), so v2 is the only shell: /api/user/ui-state still accepts and drops shell, nav_collapsed and nav_folds so open tabs get no 400, no migration, and the Hem components stay because v2 renders them.
[2026-09-13] Invoice entry row commits on pointerdown, ignores IME-composing keys, and gets a visible "Lägg till rad" button (#2447): touch fires no usable mouse event before the blur teardown and Android reports Enter as keyCode 229, so the mouse-and-Enter-only path was unreachable on Android.
[2026-09-13] Peppol per-line VAT rounding is carried in BT-114 PayableRoundingAmount at export (#2544), not by moving creation to per-category rounding: a header-only change breaks the engine balance check and a full move touches five formula copies; band = half an öre per line plus per category.
[2026-09-13] Batch booking collects possible duplicates into one confirmation dialog (#2488) instead of a skip-duplicates switch: the check still runs per row and the user decides once with each matched verifikat in view; match/ignore stay single-row as in BulkBookDialog.
[2026-09-13] WhatsApp transient link lookup gets its own M22 template, sent unthrottled (#2365): M21 is worded about a code while senders on this path are linked users sending receipts, and the fail-closed throttles read the DB that just failed, turning a blip into silence.
[2026-09-13] SKV SESSION_EXPIRED is no longer a health fault (#2567): the 65-minute BankID session is each connected company's resting state, so it left RECONSENT_ERROR_CODES, dead sessions answer from the stored row, legacy latches read as expired, the notice waits 7 days; SKV caps refreshes at 10.
[2026-09-13] Register CSV columns are scored (exact > whole word > substring) with a record-number reject list (#2548), not fixed with more vendor aliases: aliases fix one file while short keywords ("co", "vat") keep stealing columns and the confidence score hides it behind the 0.8 mapping gate.
[2026-09-13] AR reference matching derives its key set from generateOcrReference() in one helper, lib/invoices/ocr-keys.ts (#2555), not a second compare per matcher: the PDF and the matcher deriving the reference independently caused the bug, and one helper keeps print and match from drifting.
[2026-09-14] Supplier-invoice vat_treatment is enforced in the booking engine as a required argument (#2553), not only in the create routes: a route-only guard leaves the inbox-convert route, the MCP executor and every stored 0.25 row able to debit 2641.
[2026-09-13] v1 supplier mark-paid resolves payment_account as explicit > 1930 (#2550), not via company_settings.last_supplier_payment_account: that setting is the dashboard dialog's pre-fill, so reading it on the API door would silently re-point integrations that never send the field.
[2026-09-14] SIE4I identity keeps #2532's numberOmitted flag and fixes sourceId with an ordinal-based formatVoucherRef (#2546), not a retype of SIEVoucher.number to number | null: the collision (sourceId "0" for every SIE4I voucher) was the bug; that retype landed the same day in a 187-file PR.
[2026-09-13] roundOre uses a decimal-string exponent shift instead of the Number.EPSILON nudge (#2544): EPSILON is a fixed absolute quantity while the double gap doubles per power of two, so from 2 SEK up n+EPSILON===n and the helper degenerated to the naive form; a decimal library needs sign-off.
[2026-09-13] creditNoteNeedsJournalEntry moved into lib/bookkeeping/booking-mode.ts beside its supplier twin, re-exported from issue-credit-note.ts (#2552): v1 and the MCP executor each re-implemented the cash-method credit rule inline; one shared helper keeps the two sides symmetrical.
[2026-09-13] Single-side line rule enforced in engine + Zod + a NOT VALID CHECK (#2551), except on MarkInvoiceSentSchema: parseCustomIssuanceLines already owns that path and returns the targeted INVOICE_MARK_SENT_LINES_INVALID, which a schema refinement would pre-empt into a generic 400.
[2026-09-13] Enskild firma customers (#2367): the personnummer-as-org-number guard now covers only eu_business/non_eu_business, and lists mask personnummer-shaped org numbers, via one shared predicate instead of six copies; no sole_trader type (two CHECKs, enums, snapshots, VAT fork, zero gain).
[2026-09-14] Fortnox migration prefills momskod from Account.VATCode on /accounts, translated ruta-to-ruta into the account VAT treatment (#2585), Fortnox only: the SIE export the guided import runs on carries no VAT codes, so the label regex was the ceiling; Fortnox codes are named after the ruta they feed and 16 of the 38 default codes have a treatment landing on the same ruta, the rest (uttag 06, trepartshandel 37-38, import 50, and every class 2 moms code) translate to null and keep the label suggestion. Precedence is chart treatment > provider code > label, because a treatment the company set in Accounted is a deliberate later edit and the provider code is the user's own configuration in the system they are leaving. Visma (opaque VatCodeId needing a /vatcodes lookup), Björn Lundén and Briox (code sets unverified) were left out; fetchAccountingAccountsDirect answers [] for them and applySourceVatCodes takes a generic translator so each can plug in once its codes are documented.
[2026-09-14] The Fortnox momskod prefill (#2585) arrives as a SUGGESTION (unreviewed, in the momskoder-att-granska list, accepted by Bekräfta alla), not as a reviewed value as first built: syncMappedAccounts writes reviewed treatments onto existing chart accounts and the wizard skips the mapping step when nothing needs review, so a reviewed prefill would have overwritten a treatment the user cleared in Accounted on the next re-sync without anyone seeing it (skeptic refutation). The translation is ruta-exact per account number, not per code: IT (24) on a 46xx underentreprenör account answers null because Accounted files class 4 outside 4425-4427 on ruta 23, and asserting a box Fortnox disagrees with is worse than falling back to the label. Reverse-charge purchase rates come from the label (4516 12%, 4517 6% share IVEU with 4515) via vatRateFromLabel, because a flat 25% registers the account under the wrong rate bucket and the filing gate then flags every correct voucher as an rc-basis gap.
[2026-09-14] SIE wizard shows the execute rejection on the review step (inline beside the action row plus a toast) instead of pre-checking admission through a status endpoint: the server text is already the user-facing Swedish message and the pause is an operator state that can flip between render and click, so the rejection path is the only truthful one. Found when SIE_IMPORT_JOBS was unset on prod after #2532 shipped: every import since 07:50Z was refused with 400 and the wizard rendered nothing, because `error` was only passed to the upload step.
[2026-09-14] Invoice-email copies: the login-email CC fallback is removed and the company-email fallback became explicit rows (migration 20260914110000 backfills invoice_email_cc_addresses = [email] where it was NULL), so NULL and [] both mean no fixed copies; the settings UI now shows exactly what is sent. Chosen over keeping a runtime fallback because the fallback was invisible in settings (the founder saw a fixed CC he could not find). Reply-To is a new company setting (invoice_email_reply_to) with fallback company email, then the sending user's address: the sending user was already exposed as the legacy CC, and a working Reply-To is what makes "Svara direkt på detta mejl" true; cron sends (recurring, reminders) and API-key sends have no user and drop that line when no address resolves.
[2026-09-14] Whole-krona truncation rounds to öre first at five sites (#2597): ink2-engine and sruAmount call truncateToWholeKronor, three bokslut floors wrap their input in roundOre. A raw double sum filed 2495 for a ruta worth 2496 and clamped the bolagsskatt base 10 kr low.
[2026-09-14] Migration 20260914110000 was edited after its first production run failed (rolled back at the CC backfill UPDATE, never recorded in schema_migrations): the backfill now skips migration-reset source companies, whose company_settings rows block_migration_reset_source_mutation makes immutable. Edited rather than superseded because the failed file itself must succeed before Supabase applies any later version; the never-edit rule protects shipped schemas, and this one had not shipped. Preview branches carry no data, so the pg-upgrade seed is the only place a data-dependent trigger like this can be caught before main.
[2026-09-14] Recurring schedules: placeholders use the same {key} syntax and matcher as the invoice email texts (applyPlaceholders), with Swedish keys ({månad}, {nästa månad}, {föregående månad}, {år}, {periodstart}, {periodslut}, {nästa periodstart}) rather than the [current_month_name] style the user proposed: one placeholder dialect across the product. {periodslut} is the inclusive last day of the period (2026-10-01 to 2027-09-30 for a yearly schedule), with {nästa periodstart} for the exclusive form the user wrote. period_start advances after the cron run AND after "Skapa faktura nu" (unlike next_run_date, which run-now leaves alone): the invoice just created covers the period, and the stale roll-forward (no invoice) leaves it so run-now still bills the missed period. Period placeholders without a period_start are refused at save time (API + dialog) instead of rendering blank or literal on a customer invoice. Text rows on schedules mirror invoice_items.line_type; at least one product row is required.
[2026-09-14] /select-company is no longer a company picker: it renders only BankID engagements not yet in Accounted, and redirects otherwise (?choose=1 or no company: /onboarding; BankID login with a company: /). Member companies are the switcher's job; listing them again under "Lägg till företag" read as a second switcher (founder).
[2026-09-14] Books act reads the server on every load (lib/onboarding-books/resume): a running/paused import job opens the new resume step following that job; existing posted entries open on the genomlysning; only a fresh company gets "Var fanns bokföringen innan?". Replaces the source step's "du har redan N verifikat" shortcut: the act's position was browser memory while the durable job kept writing after a reload.
[2026-09-14] Books finale shares Hem's task selector and prompt, with an explicit company ID: a verified agent connection leads straight to the first actionable row. Keep manual exit and retry failed completion so the books gate cannot silently send users back into onboarding.
[2026-09-14] SIE job mappings reuse the shared four-digit account schema, including custom accounts such as 9999: the chart and worker already accept them. Keep the BAS 1-8 range for automatic suggestions only; rejecting custom targets at execution broke the preview create-account flow.
[2026-09-14] SIE custom accounts (#2605): keep unused four-digit definitions, but require report-supported targets for financial amounts before admission and preparation. Classes 0/9 lack report classification. Archive all chart pages with classification and import rules; never silently remap amounts.
[2026-09-14] SIE preview must not create targets that execution rejects: retain unused non-four-digit source definitions in the original archive with an explicit preview notice, and require deliberate target mapping for used source accounts. Keep the ledger's four-digit invariant and validate the create-accounts endpoint before any writes. Canonical validation responses expose bounded field details in both the API message and UI instead of hiding them behind the generic 400 text.
[2026-09-14] Reconcile stale OSS account constraints with a guarded migration: staging recorded the OSS migration but rejected reviewed OSS defaults. Keep the established class restrictions and leave already-correct constraints unchanged.
[2026-09-14] Legacy SIE recovery starts with a read-only, company-scoped year assessment (#2566): legacy status and year-wide counts do not prove voucher ownership, so no failed_with_data status or undo/reset permission is inferred. Resolve metadata and reviewed reversals in separate changes.
[2026-09-14] Support delivery is PostHog Support first, email fallback (lib/support/submit-feedback.ts), and the support dialog shows the conversation (components/ui/support-link.tsx): founder decided PostHog is the inbox; replies typed there were invisible because the app never rendered the thread, and the mail copy duplicated every ticket.
[2026-09-14] Onboarding progress reads committed transactions_count directly, independent of animation. Preparation stays indeterminate and final checks stay below 100%. Native SIE skips provider registers; only the selected Bokio or Visma connection is offered.
[2026-09-14] Asset defaults follow BAS 2026 non-production accounts (vehicle 1226, computer 1224, both on 1229) and vehicle/computer ranges span 1210-1269: BAS 2026 splits kontogrupp 12 by production use, and the 31 prod assets on the old 1240/1250 accounts must stay editable.
[2026-09-14] Customer list Kundnr column renders only when at least one customer has a number: the field is optional, and design.md's column budget forbids spending a permanently blank sixth column on registers that do not use numbers.
[2026-09-14] Salary runs surface as underlag references from the entry's own source_type/source_id rather than a new journal_entries column: a salary_run_id column cannot be backfilled onto posted entries (Hard Rule 1) and would duplicate a relation already stored and already read forward.
[2026-09-14] Inbox dedupe adopts a later delivery's +lev/+ver hint instead of dropping it: identical bytes are a duplicate but the sender's plus-tag is new information; applied only while the item is unconsumed (no supplier invoice, journal entry or transaction match).
[2026-09-14] WhatsApp staged media: probe Meta (lookupMedia) before releasing a parked receipt instead of trusting STAGED_MEDIA_MAX_AGE_MS: Meta returned 400 at 11 days (#2363), so the constant is a ceiling, not a retention promise; 429/5xx leave the row parked.
[2026-09-14] Invoice/article unit is free text with a shared datalist (lib/invoices/units.ts), not a closed dropdown: the API already accepts any unit up to 32 chars, so the list only hid units stored via API/import and forced a deploy per new unit.
[2026-09-14] Zettle first sync starts at the connection moment, not a 90-day BACKFILL_DAYS window; explicit POST /backfill moves the cursor rather than adding a sync_from column: the cursor already means "everything before this is handled".
[2026-09-14] v1 draft cancel = status flip via new engine cancelDraftEntry, not delete_last_voucher: the RPC gates on auth.uid() owner/admin and v1 runs service-role; drafts hold no voucher_number so cancelling leaves no lopnummer gap.
[2026-09-14] Bypassed-guard records go to audit_log (new guard-agnostic GUARD_BYPASSED action), not processing_history: behandlingshistorik is reported and archived out of audit_log (2026-08-21), so a processing_history row is invisible to the reader it exists for; the next guard needs no DDL.
[2026-09-14] Kontantmetod cut-off identity moved from journal_entries.description to a kontantmetod_cutoff_entries marker table joined by both VAT RPCs: a dedicated source_type was rejected (posted rows are immutable, so legacy rows would leave two matching paths forever).
[2026-09-14] Bokio supplier invoices: read remainingAmount, the only payment field Bokio publishes, never status/paidAmount; a zero total never declares a settlement; migrated rows are repaired by asking the provider (POST /reconcile), not by a blanket SQL update that would fabricate payments.
[2026-09-14] Onboarding drafts use scoped, expiring sessionStorage and browser history: Back and refresh retain answers without storing provider credentials or replaying completed writes; server import jobs remain authoritative.
[2026-09-14] AI handoff uses local review and explicit copy with clean external URLs: avoids URL disclosure without a new server-side prompt store. Removed the Skatteverket development token bypass instead of adding another consent exception.
[2026-09-14] Bank connect prefills Enable Banking's companyId credential from the company org number (10 digits) with credentials_autosubmit off, generic on the declared credential: Handelsbanken's hosted page asks for "Företags-ID" with no format hint and rejects the hyphen (Morfosia feedback). The signer's personnummer is never sent; we do not hold it.
[2026-09-14] A 'pending' bank_connections row older than 10 minutes renders as an abandoned attempt (retry or remove), not "Väntar på banken": Enable Banking's page can time out without ever redirecting to our callback, so no cleanup runs. Presentation only; a late callback still activates the row and the connect route sweeps it on retry.
[2026-09-14] A document attached inside the review dialog is read by the assistant right away (POST /api/agent/categorize document_id), never stored as the row's read: the row does not carry that document until booked, so a stored read would be stale at once. The dialog waits for extraction via the existing extraction-status poll.
[2026-09-15] A bank account another of the user's companies already books is shown in the picker and can be ticked, never blocked (issue #2647): the callback guard's claim is a notice, not a refusal. Rejected blocking the connect (the guard is per account, and which accounts a consent covers is only known after the BankID round trip, so a pick-time block would refuse a legitimate second company at the same bank) and a unique IBAN across companies in the schema (40 IBANs already sit in more than one company in prod, so it needs a data cleanup first). One bank account belongs to one legal entity, but the app cannot tell which of the user's companies is its right home, so it names the claimant and leaves the choice; nothing is auto-booked, the rows land unbooked and the user decides what becomes a verifikat. Founder direction.
[2026-09-15] A verifikat lists the bank and skattekonto händelser anchored to it ("Visa transaktion", desk crm#46) through a new read-only route (journal-entries/[id]/transactions) rather than by widening the underlag references resolver: a bank line is the trace of the affärshändelse, not its underlag, and folding it into references would silence "saknar underlag" for expenses that still need a kvitto. The deep link reuses /transactions?highlight, which now also switches a booked target into the history view and accepts skattekonto row ids; the reverse direction (transaction to verifikat) never existed before, the customer misremembered.
[2026-09-15] The transactions-page match dialog links one bank row to several verifikat itself (desk crm#45) instead of pointing at Bankavstämning, Matcha manuellt: the customer looked for "dela upp raden" where the row is, and the 1:N engine (linkTransactionToVouchers, #1553) already existed, the dialog only lacked the worksheet sum arithmetic (lib/reconciliation/split-selection.ts). Rejected: a Bokio-style split of the bank row into two transactions, because the row is the bank own record and splitting it breaks the statement reconciliation; the truthful shape is one row linked to N verifikat. An already-matched verifikat still cannot join a split (N:M has no engine shape), same rule as the worksheet.
[2026-09-15] INK2S 4.14 a (SRU 7763) is a third manual tax adjustment (adjustment_type deficit_carryforward) rather than a non_taxable_income row: it has its own INK2S field and its own sign, and riding on 4.5c would misfile it. The saved deficit is deducted in the bolagsskatt base and in the periodiseringsfond/SLP cap bases too, because those bases are the skattemässiga resultatet after IL 40 kap. 2 § (the year-end skill: 25 % of skattemässigt resultat), the same place the two existing manual bridges already apply.
[2026-09-16] A reference in a verifikat's free text scores OCR_REFERENCE_IN_TEXT (0.85) in both voucher matchers, behind the ocr-keys digit floor, instead of deleting the ankomstnummer arm as #2673 proposed: the weaker tier and the floor already existed, so two hand-rolled substring tests go away.
